# 项目文件作用索引

这份索引按“改什么 → 找哪个文件 → 查看方法流程”组织。C#脚本逐项说明；贴图、配置、文档也有文件条目。Unity自动维护的缓存和本机玩家数据按目录说明，不枚举每次运行都会变化的内部文件。

## 先理解整体结构

游戏启动由 `GameBootstrap` 建立实体生成入口；`WorldSessionManager` 组织世界加载和保存。`SimulationClock` 提供统一模拟时间，`EntityGeneController` 推进基因行为；实体生态组件处理生命、营养、攻击、繁殖、种群和任务。`Planet`/`Environment` 提供世界与采样，`UI` 把这些能力接到菜单、操控与管理界面。

[打开全部运行流程图](../运行流程图/README.md) · [12条业务主流程](../运行流程图/主流程.md)

## 根目录与本机目录

|目录/文件|作用|
|---|---|
|`Assets/`|Unity实际游戏内容：源码、场景、预制体、材质、配置和媒体。|
|`Assets/DarwinPark/`|本项目主要C#模块；下面按每个脚本列出职责。|
|`Assets/Resources/`|随游戏打包、按Resources路径加载的内置数据和UI资源。不是所有配置都能在发布后热改。|
|`Assets/StreamingAssets/`|按文件路径读取的媒体，当前为菜单视频与封面。|
|`Packages/`|Unity包依赖声明与锁定版本。|
|`ProjectSettings/`|Unity工程、图形、质量、物理、时间、标签和平台设置。|
|`config/`|外部游戏数值入口，当前为带注释的gameplay.json。|
|`mods/Default/`|基因树工具写出的Genes/、tree.json及entity_states.json。已有世界通常保留自身配置快照。|
|`save/<世界编号>/world.json`|每个世界的地形配置、时间、实体、基因、种群历史等持久状态。|
|`templates/entities.json`|跨世界实体模板库，供模板投放/随机生成使用。|
|`templates/spawn-conditions/*.json`|独立命名的基因条件来源；与已启用的世界比例规则快照不同。|
|`Docs/`|设计、操作、验证记录、文件索引和生成的流程图手册。|
|`Tools/`|离线资源、流程图及文件说明生成工具，不随游戏每帧运行。|
|`Artifacts/`|隔离验证工程、构建产物、截图、工具二进制和验证报告。|
|`Library/`、`Temp/`|Unity导入、编译和运行期间生成的缓存/临时内容，通常不作为功能修改入口。|
|`Logs/`|Unity导入日志及按日志配置写入的游戏日志；Windows下logs/与Logs/是同一目录名。|
|`UserSettings/`、`.vs/`|本机编辑器布局/偏好与Visual Studio缓存。游戏改键另外保存在PlayerPrefs。|
|`.git/`|Git版本历史和索引。|
|`*.csproj`、`*.slnx`|IDE/编译入口；Unity生成的csproj应由工程同步更新。|

## 按修改目标找文件

|想修改的内容|先看这些文件|
|---|---|
|行动/基础饱食消耗|`config/gameplay.json` → `GameplayTuning.cs` → `EntityVitals.cs`。|
|默认实体基因|`Assets/Resources/Entities/entity_default.json`、`EntityFactory.cs`、`EntityGeneController.cs`。|
|基因定义与进化关系|`Assets/Resources/Genes/`、`Genetics/default_tree.json`、`GeneTreeStorage.cs`、`GeneLogicRegistry.cs`。|
|普通/孤雌繁殖|`ReproductionGeneLogic.cs`、`ReproductionEvolutionUpgrades.cs`、`ecology_balance.json`及`parthenogenesis.json`。|
|王后、工匠和补给|`AntColonyMember.cs`、`AntColonyWorker.cs`、`AntColonyReproduction.cs`、`ColonyFeeding.cs`及`ant_colony.json`。|
|攻击、尸体与营养守恒|`EntityCombat.cs`、`EntityDeathSettlement.cs`、`NutritionTransfer.cs`、`ColonyResource.cs`。|
|营养寄生/卵寄生|`NutritionParasite.cs`、`EggHostPregnancy.cs`及`HeterotrophyGeneLogic.cs`；旧兼容入口不要与新逻辑混淆。|
|种群活动范围和外观|`ColonyActivityRange.cs`、`PopulationRangeLayer.cs`、`PopulationRangeOverlay.hlsl`、`PopulationArchive.cs`。|
|随机生成和比例维持|`RandomSpawnWorkbench.cs`、`RandomPlanetSpawner.cs`、`PopulationMaintenance.cs`、`RandomSpawnPresetStore.cs`。|
|菜单/底栏/控件风格|`GameMainMenu.cs`、`SimulationControlPanel.cs`、`UIFactory.cs`、`UIVisualTheme.cs`、`RuntimeMenuTheme.cs`及`UI/theme.json`。|
|改键与实体操控|`ControlBindings.cs`、`ControlSettingsPage.cs`、`EntityPossessionInput.cs`、`EntityManualControl.cs`。|
|世界存档|`WorldSessionManager.cs`、`WorldSaveStore.cs`、`EntitySaveState.cs`、`ColonyWorldState.cs`。|
|地形、气候和矿脉|`PlanetTerrainGenerator.cs`、`PlanetTerrainMeshBuilder.cs`、`PlanetClimateModel.cs`、`PlanetOreSystem.cs`。|
|局域网权限与同步|`LanSession.cs`、`LanEditActions.cs`、`LanProtocol.cs`、`LanWorldSnapshot.cs`。|
|日志和诊断|`RuntimeFileLog.cs`、`SessionLogWriter.cs`、`RuntimeCrashDiagnostics.cs`及runtime_logging/diagnostics配置。|

## 各模块C#脚本

“运行时”表示可被游戏使用，不证明每个声明都在当前场景主动执行；具体挂载、创建和调用看流程图。Editor目录是测试/工具入口，不是玩家每帧逻辑。

### GameRoot：启动与实体装配（2个）

|文件|作用|流程/源码|
|---|---|---|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/GameRoot/EntityFactory.cs](../../Assets/DarwinPark/GameRoot/EntityFactory.cs)|从预制体或基础对象创建实体，安装基因、生命、物理与生态组件。|[查看](../运行流程图/模块/GameRoot.md#s54fcd89ef79d04f6)|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/GameRoot/GameBootstrap.cs](../../Assets/DarwinPark/GameRoot/GameBootstrap.cs)|场景启动入口：注册实体生成委托并初始化基因数据库。|[查看](../运行流程图/模块/GameRoot.md#sb97bb0ddbd6571c6)|

## 服务端运维入口

|文件|作用|
|---|---|
|[Server/Bootstrap.ps1](../../Server/Bootstrap.ps1)|校验和释放便携核心，保留配置并进入开服控制台。|
|[Server/Console.ps1](../../Server/Console.ps1)|读取数据目录配置启动真实服务端，显示实时日志并提供状态查询和保存停服控制台。|
|[Server/PortableStart.cmd](../../Server/PortableStart.cmd)|嵌入启动器的两文件开服入口模板。|
|[Server/Start.cmd](../../Server/Start.cmd)|核心包控制台启动入口，调用系统 PowerShell 并在错误退出时保留窗口。|
|[Server/Start.ps1](../../Server/Start.ps1)|手工启动无界面专服并指定持久数据目录。|
|[Server/Stop.ps1](../../Server/Stop.ps1)|请求服务端保存停服，更新需等待成功凭证和租约释放。|
|[Server/server.example.json](../../Server/server.example.json)|独立专服的初始配置示例，不覆盖已有配置。|

## 内置与外部配置

|文件|作用|
|---|---|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/ant_colony.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/ant_colony.json)|蚁群角色、营养、补给、育幼、巡逻、建群和王后繁殖参数。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/biology.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/biology.json)|配置数据：Assets/Resources/Entities/biology.json；字段与约束见对应加载脚本及流程图。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/colony_formation.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/colony_formation.json)|成员站位间距、避让与出生空位尝试参数。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_balance.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_balance.json)|生产/消费者的代谢、能量预算、孕育和食物压力参数。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_defaults.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_defaults.json)|内置生态比例目标70/30、基础繁衍与自养行动种源模板、有限建群保障；不触发持续补员。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_performance.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/ecology_performance.json)|生态邻域、缓存和批处理等性能相关参数。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/energy_levels.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/energy_levels.json)|五级蓝绿黄橙红能量阈值、色值和大小的内置默认表。|
|[Assets/Resources/Entities/entity_default.json](../../Assets/Resources/Entities/entity_default.json)|默认实体的初始基因组合及数值。|

|[config/simulation_optimization.json](../../config/simulation_optimization.json)|可外部调整并热加载的任务队列、查询周期、缓存、接地与外观优化参数。|

## Shader与HLSL

|文件|作用|
|---|---|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Atmosphere.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Atmosphere.shader)|大气效果着色器；不要仅按相似文件名判断当前使用情况。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetAtmosphere.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetAtmosphere.shader)|大气效果着色器，实际启用由材质/天气组件决定。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetCloudVolume.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetCloudVolume.shader)|体积云噪声、密度和视线积分。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetOcean.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetOcean.shader)|海水颜色、光照与海面上的数据/种群范围图层。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetScattering.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetScattering.shader)|大气散射、透明混合和真实深度遮挡。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetSurface.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetSurface.shader)|基础星球表面顶点色与光照着色器。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetURP.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/PlanetURP.shader)|URP星球地表主着色器，结合高度、气候、矿脉、细节和种群范围。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetDayNight.hlsl](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetDayNight.hlsl)|按球面太阳高度计算昼夜亮度，复用全局配置且不增加纹理采样。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetNearSurfaceDetail.hlsl](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetNearSurfaceDetail.hlsl)|近景地面颗粒与细节混合辅助函数。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetSurfaceDetail.hlsl](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PlanetSurfaceDetail.hlsl)|地表多频噪声和细节颜色辅助函数。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PopulationRangeOverlay.hlsl](../../Assets/DarwinPark/Planet/Terrain/PopulationRangeOverlay.hlsl)|同群活动圆先求并集，再绘制半透明填色或外沿，支持各群不透明度。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/UI/OrganismParts.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/UI/OrganismParts.shader)|合并生物部件的实例颜色、生命周期及日照着色。|
|[Assets/DarwinPark/UI/PlanetGlobeMinimap.shader](../../Assets/DarwinPark/UI/PlanetGlobeMinimap.shader)|GPU视觉着色资源：Assets/DarwinPark/UI/PlanetGlobeMinimap.shader；实际函数与渲染分支见流程图。|

## 材质、渲染与媒体资源

|文件|作用|
|---|---|

## 工程、依赖与仓库入口

|文件|作用|
|---|---|
|[.gitattributes](../../.gitattributes)|规定文本换行、Git LFS、Unity合并与生成文档标记。|
|[.gitignore](../../.gitignore)|规定缓存、生成物及本机玩家数据不进入版本控制。|
|[.vsconfig](../../.vsconfig)|Visual Studio开发工作负载/组件建议。|
|[CHANGELOG.md](../../CHANGELOG.md)|项目功能与维护变更记录。|
|[Darwin_paradise.slnx](../../Darwin_paradise.slnx)|IDE与dotnet构建使用的解决方案入口，组织各C#项目。|
|[Packages/manifest.json](../../Packages/manifest.json)|声明Unity Package Manager依赖及要求版本。|
|[Packages/packages-lock.json](../../Packages/packages-lock.json)|锁定已解析依赖及其间接依赖版本。|
|[ProjectSettings/AudioManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/AudioManager.asset)|全局音频设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/ClusterInputManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/ClusterInputManager.asset)|Unity集群输入设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/DynamicsManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/DynamicsManager.asset)|Unity三维物理全局设置；星球实体另有PlanetGravity。|
|[ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset)|打包场景清单与构建相关场景信息。|
|[ProjectSettings/EditorSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/EditorSettings.asset)|编辑器资源序列化等工程选项。|
|[ProjectSettings/GraphicsSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/GraphicsSettings.asset)|全局图形与渲染管线引用。|
|[ProjectSettings/InputManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/InputManager.asset)|旧输入系统的轴/按钮定义；自定义改键另见ControlBindings。|
|[ProjectSettings/MemorySettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/MemorySettings.asset)|Unity内存与分配设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/MultiplayerManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/MultiplayerManager.asset)|Unity多人服务相关工程设置，不等于自建LAN协议参数。|
|[ProjectSettings/NavMeshAreas.asset](../../ProjectSettings/NavMeshAreas.asset)|导航网格区域定义。|
|[ProjectSettings/PackageManagerSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/PackageManagerSettings.asset)|包管理器工程设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/Physics2DSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/Physics2DSettings.asset)|Unity二维物理设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/PresetManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/PresetManager.asset)|默认组件预设设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset)|产品、平台、Player等工程级设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt](../../ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt)|本项目期望的Unity编辑器版本。|
|[ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset)|画质档位及关联的渲染设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/SceneTemplateSettings.json](../../ProjectSettings/SceneTemplateSettings.json)|Unity场景模板设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/ShaderGraphSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/ShaderGraphSettings.asset)|Shader Graph编辑设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/TagManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/TagManager.asset)|标签、层和排序层定义，影响遮罩与拾取。|
|[ProjectSettings/TimeManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/TimeManager.asset)|Unity固定物理步和全局时间参数；游戏日期由SimulationClock控制。|
|[ProjectSettings/URPProjectSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/URPProjectSettings.asset)|URP工程级设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/UnityConnectSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/UnityConnectSettings.asset)|Unity账号/云服务相关工程设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/VFXManager.asset](../../ProjectSettings/VFXManager.asset)|Visual Effect Graph相关工程设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/VersionControlSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/VersionControlSettings.asset)|Unity版本控制集成设置。|
|[ProjectSettings/XRSettings.asset](../../ProjectSettings/XRSettings.asset)|XR相关工程设置。|
|[README.md](../../README.md)|项目介绍、运行方式和主要功能入口。|
|[agent.md](../../agent.md)|代码、配置、回归检查和Bug记录的项目开发规范。|

## 配套文件与容易混淆的地方

- **`.meta`**：当前版本控制中有697个。它们保存Unity资源GUID和导入设置，作用属于对应资源/目录，不是额外游戏功能；本索引不为每个副本重复一行。
- **配置文件与存档快照不同**：Resources为内置默认，config/gameplay.json为外部调参，mods为编辑后的基因内容，world.json为某个世界已经保存的状态。不要假定只改一处就能改变所有旧档。
- **同类文件不一定是当前主实现**：例如Planet/Legacy内OreGenerator/OreNode只为旧恢复场景保留GUID兼容，正式场景当前主矿脉看PlanetOreSystem；旧ParasiticGeneLogic/ParasiteBehavior不要当作新的NutritionParasite。Shader实际选用由材质和天气组件决定。
- **一个文件可能有多个类**：HeterotrophyGeneLogic.cs还包含食腐/卵寄生实现，GeneLogicRegistry.cs也定义IGeneLogic接口；logicScriptId不保证与文件名相同。
- **界面与逻辑分开**：按钮界面负责编辑/提交请求，具体生成、繁殖、伤害与权限检查应追到Entities和LanSession等实际执行入口。
- **流程图分图**：`Docs/运行流程图/模块/*.md`按模块合并，脚本和方法使用独立锚点，统一作用是保存该文件的方法图与调用链接，已由上面的“查看”列逐个关联。
- **更新方式**：新增或改动文件职责时修改 `Tools/file_catalog_roles.json`，再运行 `python Tools/generate_file_catalog.py`。流程图的一键更新也会同步刷新文件索引。
